A robustness check with a second model and scaffold reproduced these failures. We release the expert reviews, survey responses, agent repositories, and logs. Our results provide early evidence that today's agents can do the engineering of AI research, but struggle with critical parts of the research lifecycle.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Bitcoin Runs on a Clock: Why Every Price Indicator Dies and the Halving Clock Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26188)
*Josh Molnar*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文发现比特币长期有效的周期指标（Pi Cycle、MVRV等）在近三个周期中系统性退化：从精确→提前→失效。通过固定机械规则识别，顶部在减半后525-546天高度聚集（统计显著性p<5e-6），底部在顶部后364-406天。唯一稳定的信号是时间自创世以来的幂律关系（指数~5.6），在以太坊和另一数据源上复现。研究预注册了2026年底部和下次减半后顶部的时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币广泛使用的周期指标在过去十年一直精准，但最近开始退化（提前、沉默），作者试图理解这一系统性现象，并寻找真正稳定的周期结构。

Method: 使用固定机械规则识别周期顶部和底部，进行多种统计检验（联合聚类p值、块自助法仿真、旋转零假设检验），并测试宏观因子（M2、收益率曲线）和幂律模型。

Result: 顶部聚类高度显著（0/10,000自助法复现），底部部分由回撤过程本身决定（31-43%可复现）。幂律时间模型（指数5.6）是唯一信号稳定且跨资产复现的指标，当前周期已跑赢买入持有。

Conclusion: 比特币周期的顶部时间结构是真实且可预测的（减半后525-546天），但传统阈值指标因结构性退化已失效。预注册了2026年底部（10月5日-11月16日）和下次顶部窗口作为可证伪预测。

Abstract: Every widely followed Bitcoin cycle indicator (Pi Cycle, MVRV, Mayer, Puell) called turns precisely for a decade, then degraded in one sequence: precise, then early, then silent. This is one structural phenomenon. Across the four halving epochs (2011-2026), the per-cycle maxima of five top-calling oscillators decline monotonically while minima end higher, so any threshold calibrated on past cycles must stop firing; short-horizon indicators decay toward zero and several invert sign; yet Bitcoin's time structure stays fixed, with mature-cycle tops 525/546/534 days after their halvings and bottoms 406/364/366 days after their tops. Turns are identified retrospectively by a fixed mechanical rule, not a real-time record. Timing-free nulls put the joint clustering at 5e-6 to 1e-3 across every variant. A harder empirical null (block-bootstrapped paths under the identical rule) never reproduces the top cluster under its deterministic construction (0 of 10,000); the bottom cluster is largely intrinsic to the drawdown process (31-43% of paths), so the evidence concentrates in top phase-alignment. In block height (the exact 210,000-block unit) the top null stays 0 of 10,000 and partial bottom structure emerges; shape and volatility overlays do not improve. A causal power law in time-since-genesis (exponent near 5.6) is the only signal whose sign is stable across mature epochs, replicates on a second source and Ethereum, and whose timing edge over buy-and-hold turns positive in the current cycle (one holdout, suggestive not decisive). We rest nothing on per-epoch significance: a rotation null shows HAC inference over-rejects here (size 0.33 at nominal 0.05; p=0.21). Macro drivers (M2, yield curve) show the same instability and lose a joint horse race. We pre-register falsifiable windows: a 2026 bottom (Oct 5-Nov 16) and a next top 525-546 days after the following halving.

</details>


### [180] [Where does the criticality live? Early-warning signals are event-heterogeneous across seven crypto-perpetual liquidation cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27070)
*Ramon Marc Garcia Seuma*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究加密货币永续合约清算级联是否具有临界转变的早期预警指纹，发现价格变量在5/7事件中表现出临界减速信号，但突发新闻冲击事件中信号消失，表明存在内生积累型与外生冲击型两类级联结构。唯一的稳健规律是吃单订单流方差压缩，但只能作为群体水平的前兆而非单事件预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究加密货币永续合约清算级联是否具有可复现的临界转变早期预警指纹，以及该指纹存在于哪个状态变量中，以区分内生积累型与外生冲击型两类清算事件。

Method: 基于2022-2025年七次BTC清算级联事件（含2025年10月10日创纪录的190亿美元事件），使用分钟级价格数据和5分钟级杠杆/订单流数据，对去趋势残差计算滚动方差和滞后1自相关，采用Kendall-tau统计检验其前兆趋势，每个事件中每个变量共扫描39种分析配置。

Result: 没有变量在所有事件中一致有效。价格在5/7事件中呈现临界减速信号，但在两次突发新闻（关税）冲击事件中完全沉默，表明存在两类级联结构。2025年10月事件中信号存在于杠杆而非价格，但属于异常值而非普遍规律。唯一稳健的规律是吃单订单流方差压缩，该规律通过300次起点的安慰剂检验（Fisher组合p值约5e-6），但仅作为群体前兆而非单事件预警。

Conclusion: 加密货币衍生品中基于单事件的临界减速断言本质上脆弱。模式失效本身具有诊断价值：临界减速恰好出现在失稳机制最突然的事件中缺失，这与这些级联属于不连续的、冲击驱动的转变而非临界转变的预期一致。

Abstract: Do crypto perpetual-futures crashes carry a reproducible early-warning fingerprint of a critical transition, and in which state variable? We study seven major BTC liquidation cascades (2022-2025, including the record 19B USD event of 10 October 2025) using minute-level price and 5-minute leverage/order-flow data. On detrended residuals we compute rolling variance and lag-1 autocorrelation and test their pre-cascade trend with the Kendall-tau statistic, sweeping 39 analysis configurations per variable per event. No variable is event-invariant. Price carries the critical-slowing-down signature in five of seven events but is silent in exactly the two sudden-news (tariff) shocks, suggesting a two-type structure: endogenous-buildup versus exogenous-shock cascades. The October 2025 event, whose in-sample analysis suggests the signature lives in leverage rather than price, turns out to be the outlier, not the rule. The one regularity surviving all events with data is a compression of taker order-flow variance, which passes a 300-onset placebo test (Fisher-combined p ~ 5e-6) but is a population-level precursor, not a per-event alarm. Single-event critical-slowing-down claims in crypto derivatives are therefore fragile by construction. We argue the pattern of failures is itself diagnostic: slowing down is absent exactly where the destabilising mechanism is most abrupt, as one would expect if these cascades are discontinuous, shock-driven transitions rather than critical ones.

</details>
